  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2023年PTA合作组发现纳赫兹引力波背景证据后,下一个主要目标是表征引力波背景并识别其源。传统PTA分析中使用的贝叶斯因子(BF)计算方法(如乘积空间采样和热力学积分)在比较不相似或非嵌套模型时计算成本高昂且效率低下,亟需开发高效的模型比较方法。

Method: 提出了一种结合归一化流、嵌套采样和边缘化PTA似然函数的模型比较方法。使用新开发的Python包coppuccino训练归一化流,该包学习数据的copula结构(转换为均匀边缘分布后的依赖结构)。似然函数对所有非引力波背景参数进行边缘化处理,从而实现高效的模型比较。

Result: 在解析目标和模拟PTA数据上验证了该方法。解析证据的恢复结果在误差范围内,将贝叶斯因子作为注入模型的二元分类器时,曲线下面积(AUC)得分达到0.996。该方法能够在消费级硬件上以分钟为单位完成任意引力波背景谱模型的贝叶斯模型比较。

Conclusion: 成功开发了一种快速、高效的PTA引力波背景模型比较方法,显著降低了计算成本和时间,使得在普通硬件上进行复杂模型比较成为可能。该方法为引力波背景源的识别和表征提供了强大的分析工具,对推进纳赫兹引力波研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: In 2023, pulsar timing array (PTA) collaborations around the world announced evidence for a nanohertz gravitational-wave background (GWB). Beyond increasing the detection significance, the next major goal is to characterize the GWB and identify its source(s). The typical model-comparison metric used in PTA analyses is the Bayes factor (BF). However, BF computation methods used in the PTA literature, such as product-space sampling and thermodynamic integration, are computationally expensive or inefficient if the models compared are dissimilar or non-nested. In this work, we introduce a PTA model-comparison method using a combination of normalizing flows, nested sampling, and a PTA likelihood that is marginalized over all non-GWB parameters. The flows are trained using a newly-developed Python package coppuccino, which learns the data's copula: the dependence structure after transforming to uniform marginals. We validate the method on both analytic targets and simulated PTA data, recovering the analytic evidence within error bars and achieving an area-under-the-curve (AUC) score of 0.996 when treating the BFs as binary classifiers of the injected models. The result is a method to perform Bayesian model comparison of arbitrary GWB spectral models in minutes on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [134] [Polar-metric/axial-gauge perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33511)
*Abhishake Sadhukhan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov (MMP)可穿越虫洞的线性扰动，在磁荷背景下推导了耦合的Zerilli-Moncrief型主方程，证明了喉部区域的自洽性要求费米子对扰动的完整响应（包括MMP舍弃的应力张量迹部分）以及诱导霍尔电流，并在费米子反作用参数α的一阶近似下给出了扰动模式的修正解和频谱分裂，确认该扇区在l≥2时无指数增长模式。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMP可穿越虫洞作为量子引力的重要理论模型，其稳定性需要通过线性扰动分析来检验。前人工作中MMP舍弃了应力张量的迹部分，本研究旨在完整考虑费米子响应（包括二维共形反常的完整贡献）及其诱导的霍尔电流，以严格验证虫洞喉部方程在扰动下的自洽性，并研究费米子反作用对扰动模式频谱和稳定性的影响。

Method: 在磁荷背景上建立极坐标度规扰动与轴向规范场扰动的耦合系统，将其化简为两个耦合的Zerilli-Moncrief型主方程。虫洞分为两个区域分析：嘴部采用近极端磁荷Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞几何；喉部利用带电费米子最低朗道能级的卡西米尔能量维持开放，从二维共形反常精确计算费米子对扰动的完整应力张量响应（包括迹部分），并从有效作用量推导诱导霍尔电流（引入系数为c/48π的欧拉项）。在α=0时喉部为精确的AdS₂×S²，扰动解耦为质量参数l(l-1)和(l+1)(l+2)的两个Poschl-Teller问题；利用摄动论给出α的一阶修正的闭合形式解，在重叠区域匹配嘴部和喉部解。

Result: 证明了喉部方程的自洽性严格要求包含费米子的完整应力张量响应和诱导霍尔电流，两者同时存在时线性化的Bianchi恒等式在一阶α成立。霍尔电流的推导需要有效作用量中包含Euler项，对应磁通量与曲率的耦合（类似Wen-Zee项）。在α=0时扰动模式解耦为两个Poschl-Teller问题，能级频率间隔为1/ℓ。给出了O(α)修正的闭合解，修正项混合两个模式且依赖于频率，在重叠区域与嘴部解匹配。势的对称部分在整个虫洞中为正，排除纯指数增长。α的修正降低本征频率并分裂原本简并的能级，一阶频移为实数，该扇区在l≥2时无增长模式。

Conclusion: 通过完整包含二维共形反常的应力张量迹部分和诱导霍尔电流，建立了MMP可穿越虫洞线性扰动理论的自洽框架。费米子反作用的一阶修正导致扰动模式的频率降低和能级分裂，但势在整个虫洞保持正定，在l≥2的扇区中不存在指数增长的不稳定模式，为虫洞在该扇区的稳定性提供了理论支持。研究揭示了量子场论效应（共形反常、霍尔响应）对虫洞几何扰动的深刻影响，以及拓扑项（Euler项、类Wen-Zee项）在维持理论自洽性中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study linear perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov (MMP) traversable wormhole. On a magnetically charged background, polar metric perturbations couple to axial perturbations of the gauge field. We treat this sector and reduce it to two coupled master equations of Zerilli-Moncrief type. The wormhole has two regions. In the mouths the geometry is a nearly extremal magnetic Reissner-Nordstrom black hole with no quantum source. In the throat the Casimir energy of the lowest Landau level of charged fermions holds the wormhole open. We show that the throat equations are consistent only if the fermions respond to the perturbation, computed exactly from the two-dimensional conformal anomaly with the full stress tensor including the trace part that MMP discard. This response must be accompanied by an induced Hall current of the fermions. With both effects included, the linearised Bianchi identities hold at first order in the fermionic backreaction $α$. Deriving the Hall current from the fermion effective action instead requires an Euler term with coefficient $c/48π$, which becomes a coupling between magnetic flux and curvature similar to a Wen-Zee term. At $α=0$ the throat is exact AdS$_2\times S^2$ and the modes decouple into two Poschl-Teller problems with masses $l(l-1)$ and $(l+1)(l+2)$, with levels spaced by $1/\ell$ in frequency. We give the $O(α)$ corrections in closed form. They mix the two modes and depend on frequency, and match the mouths in the overlap region. The symmetric part of the potential is positive throughout the wormhole, excluding purely exponential growth. The corrections lower the normal frequencies and split the levels shared by the two modes, with real shifts at first order in $α$. This sector has no growing mode for $l\ge2$ at this order.

</details>
